### Experience Matters

We don’t just know the market — we helped build it.

Reno Realty is led by a fifth-generation Nevadan with deep roots in the local community and over two decades of experience in Northern Nevada real estate. After graduating from the University of Nevada, Reno, our broker began a career specializing in residential and distressed property sales, quickly becoming a top-producing agent at one of the region’s most established brokerages.

With experience in large-scale default disposition, investment strategy, and multifamily management, our leadership brings a rare combination of local insight and institutional knowledge to every transaction. We’re proud to offer a team-focused, client-first approach to sales, leasing, and asset management throughout Reno, Sparks, Lake Tahoe, and beyond.

# Buyers

January 26, 2015

# Buying Your New Home

**Reno Realty makes the home-buying process an Easy Experience.**

Whatever stage of the home-buying process you are in, we are here to help. Our guides help educate you on the process of purchasing your new home with Reno Realty. We’ve broken down our guides based on your experience, follow our easy guides below:

**What best describes your knowledge of real estate in Northern Nevada?**

-   _We’re renting and curious about how to purchase a home (and the benefits of home ownership!)._
-   _I’m a first-time home buyer, I have never bought a home before._
-   _We used to own a home, but I lost it through foreclosure (or a short sale)._
-   _I already own a home, but I’d like to purchase a new home._
-   _I’m already a homeowner and would like to learn how to buy a rental property._
-   _I’d like to learn how to flip homes._

With our extraordinary resources, staff, and professionalism, we’ll help you find the property you are looking for! Search below many of our local markets or _**[customize your own search](https://www.reno-realty.com/property-search/)**_. If you are looking for more information on home buying, call us, and we’ll be more than happy to help you out!

# Instant Email Alerts

January 26, 2015

Reno Realty offers a valuable tool to their customers and clients, Instant MLS updates via email. This service is free and can be as detailed or broad as the customer wishes. If you are interested in purchasing a property, it is highly recommended that you contact one of the Reno Realty Realtor® to start receiving instant MLS updates, another valuable exclusive service Reno Realty. Please complete the form below and one of our knowledgable Realtors® will be in touch with you to provide a custom tailored Instant MLS Update Email Search for you.

## About

### Here's a little history about Reno Realty

-   ![](https://www.reno-realty.com/wp-content/themes/renorealty/img/agency/about/1.jpg)
    
    #### 2009-2011
    
    #### The dawn of time.
    
    A recession born company, led by Kristopher Kent, Real Estate Services of Nevada, Ltd was formed by top producers who were tired of working for large franchises where commissions are nickled and dimed.
    
-   ![](https://www.reno-realty.com/wp-content/themes/renorealty/img/agency/about/2.jpg)
    
    #### Summer 2011
    
    #### The Brokerage expands
    
    Real Estate Services of Nevada, Ltd launches its vacation rental & property management department on boarding 125+ rentals during the summer of 2011.
    
-   ![](https://www.reno-realty.com/wp-content/themes/renorealty/img/agency/about/3.jpg)
    
    #### Spring 2012
    
    #### New Homes Expansion
    
    Real Estate Services of Nevada, Ltd expands the company into new home sales, on-boarding New Home developments.
    
-   ![](https://www.reno-realty.com/wp-content/themes/renorealty/img/agency/about/4.jpg)
    
    #### Fall 2013
    
    #### Phase 3 Expansion
    
    Real Estate Services of Nevada, Ltd hires Greg Anastassatos to expand into multi-family and investment properties.
    
-   ![](https://www.reno-realty.com/wp-content/themes/renorealty/img/agency/about/5.jpg)
    
    #### Fall 2014
    
    #### Reno Real Estate,  
    Reinvigorated.
    
    After 7 years, Real Estate Services of Nevada, Ltd reinvigorates the Full Service Brokerage Model, finalizing its name change to Reno Realty and moving into their flagship office in the heart of Reno, NV.
    
-   #### Be Part  
    Of Our  
    Story!

## Compensation

We have 3 Plans: New Agent, Part-Time Agent, & Experienced Agent

### Experienced Agent

80% Agent **/** 20% Brokerage on your first $62,500 in gross commissions in your contract year (the cap), thereafter you keep 100% (less a 5% pinnacle fee\*) until the end of your contract year.

-   A $50 File Admin Fee Is deducted out of each transaction side.
-   Experienced Agents must hit the cap every year or they **may** be downgraded to the Part Time Agent Plan the next calendar year (at Broker’s discretion).
-   All plans reset annually based on the Agent’s contract year.
-   All forms of income are applied towards the Agent's gross commission split, i.e. referrals, BPO's, etc.
-   All Agent Plans require a $65 monthly subscription fee for office/technology.

### Part Time Agent

70% Agent **/** 30% Brokerage on your first $41,667 in gross commissions in your contract year (the cap), thereafter you keep 100% (less a 10% pinnacle fee\*) until the end of your contract year.

-   Part Time Agent is defined as an agent that does not cap. Upon capping, you will be promoted to the Experienced Agent plan for the next contract year.
-   A $50 File Admin Fee Is deducted out of each transaction side.
-   All forms of income are applied towards the Agent’s gross commission split, i.e. referrals, BPO's, etc.
-   A Part Time Agent that caps is automatically upgraded to Experienced Agent plan the following year.
-   An Agent that does not meet the cap shall remain on the Part Time Agent plan the following contract year.
-   All plans reset annually based on the Agent’s contract year.
-   All Agent Plans require a $65 monthly subscription fee for office/technology.

### New Agent

60% Agent **/** 40% Brokerage on your first real estate sales transaction, thereafter (and all other income) is at a 70% Agent **/** 30% Brokerage (Gross Commission Split) on your next $30,000 in gross commissions in your contract year (the cap), thereafter you keep 100% (less a 15% pinnacle fee\*) until the end of your contract year.

-   New Agent is defined as an agent that is brand new agent without any experience. Contact the Broker for exceptions.
-   Upon capping, you will be promoted to the Experienced Agent plan for the next contract year.
-   All forms of income are applied towards the Agent's gross commission split, i.e. referrals, BPO's, etc.
-   A $50 File Admin Fee Is deducted out of each transaction side.
-   New & Part Time Agents that cap are automatically upgraded to the Experienced Agent Plan the following year.
-   An Agent that does not meet the cap shall be placed on the Part Time Agent plan the following contract year.
-   All plans reset annually based on the Agent's contract year.
-   All Agent Plans require a $65 monthly subscription fee for office/technology.

### Teams

Please contact us if you are a team or thinking of joining as a team as we have custom plans for legitimate Real Estate Teams.

  

\***Pinnical Fees** are fees that kick in when an agent caps, these fees cover our incurred expenses on your behalf, E&O Insurance Premiums, Advertising, Operations, etc.

Close Window

## Workspace

We are in a historic office space right in the heart of Reno, NV.

Running a business is expensive—luckily we have been able to negotiate great rates on top of the line Technology & premier Office space. Here are a few of the benefits or our workspace:

-   Free Copies, Printing & Coffee.
-   Your own dedicated phone number (Plus call forwarding, find me/follow me and more, fax to email).
-   Cubes with fully loaded workstations featuring Windows 10.
-   weekly sales/achievment meetings.
-   Regularly scheduled company events.
-   Broker Breakfasts, Lunches, Dinners and Community Involvement.
-   Walking distance from downtown and lots of great restaurants.

  
Close Window

## Agent Assistance

Running a business is hard work. Here at Reno Realty, you are never alone. With our guidance, we'll help you navigate the industry with tools that will ensure your success.

Here are some of the tools for our Agents:

-   Productivity/Accountability Sessions
-   Email Blasts and Drip Marketing Campaigns
-   Sphere of Influence Building
-   Weekly sales/achievement meetings.
-   Regularly scheduled company events.

  
Close Window

## Broker Support

With an experienced broker right at your fingertips anytime, you'll be able to handle any transaction like a pro—even if you’re just starting out. Never be embarrassed again by not having all the answers. You'll always be able to provide accurate information quickly to your clients.

Here are just a few of the things our Broker will do with you:

-   Broker Productivity/Accountability Sessions
-   Broker Training
-   Broker Meetings.
-   Regularly Broker Events

  
Close Window

## Technology

Let's face it, technology is continuing to advance and with the release of a new iPhone every single year, it can be hard to keep up! Here at Reno Realty, we've employed some of the smartest minds to build a software platform that works. Through a combination of existing enterprise software solutions and proprietary application, we've built a powerful solution that will allow you to focus on your business while leaving the technology to us.

-   Reno-Realty.com Our flagship website receives hundreds of thousands of visits each year. With thousands of active listings, dynamically changing content and consumer focused content, your buyers and sellers will enjoy using our website as a tool, in turn translating into your success.
-   Reno-Realty.com/blog Several times each week, we'll feature new content about local developments, news and market statistics. This has cultivated a following of local residents, we encourage every agent to write for our blog as this allows you the opportunity to become an expert in the local community and keeps you as a top of mind candidate for prospective clients in the community.
-   Markmail Our exclusive/proprietary online platform (developed by REDOTI) allows our agents to send marketing emails (in the masses), ensure transaction compliance (never forget a document for your file!) and works side by side with the local MLS to provide you with data for your Sellers, scheduling and mapping of your showings. Markmail, like the name suggests, also provides tools for your direct mailers for your farm area and sphere of influence!
-   We also provide our agents with Google Apps along with a portal to access office phone and fax, making changes and keeping track of calls and faxes!

  
Close Window

# Greg Anastassatos

January 26, 2015

Greg received his [Masters in Business Administration (MBA)](http://www.unr.edu/degrees/business-administration/mba) from the [University of Nevada](http://unr.edu) in 2012. His education and experience make him Reno Realty’s Investment Specialist. Using technical investment analysis calculations and tactics, Greg is able to use these and other procedures to maximize return on investment for his clients. Whether you are a first time buyer or looking to sell your multi-family property, Greg has the experience and education you demand for great success. Contact [Greg Anastassatos](mailto:greg@reno-realty.com) today for a free consultation.

# Kristopher Kent

January 26, 2015

Kristopher Kent is the proud broker of Reno Realty. Reno Realty is Reno’s premier full service real estate brokerage, offering residential, multifamily and small to mid-size commercial real estate services. Since 2009 Reno Realty has been working with both buyers & sellers in their purchase, sale and lease of their property. We strive to offer the highest quality of service, bringing state of the art technology, high quality marketing that has earned the business of the listing the nicest homes in Reno, NV. Reno Realty is not just in Reno, NV, but represent clients in all of Northwestern Nevada, including but not limited to Sparks, Washoe Valley, Fernley, Dayton, Gardnerville, Carson City, Lake Tahoe, Zephyr Cove, Incline Village, Yerington, Hawthorne and beyond.

Mr. Kent has been licensed since 2007 and has completed over 400 transactions within the State of Nevada during his career. Currently Mr. Kent represents primarily residential buyers in the purchase of their homes and sellers in the listing/marketing for sale of their homes. In addition to client representation, Kristopher enjoys teaching and training the team of Realtors at Reno Realty in the fundamentals of his experience in real estate.

Kristopher’s past work includes large volume disposition and/or acquisition of distressed properties in California and Nevada. His clients ranged from institutional investor’s (REIT’s, Wall Street Firms), Banks (Wells Fargo, Bank of America, etc.), Mortgage Originators (PHH Mortgage), Loan Servicers (Nationstar Mortgage,OCWEN Loan Servicing etc.) and Government Sponsored Entities (Fannie Mae, etc.). In addition to his exceptional work as a REO Broker, he has managed 125+ vacation rental properties through an independent affiliate of Reno Realty. Kristopher began his real estate career working closely with his Grandfather managing a large family trust including residential, multi-family, retail and industrial properties.

### 2 Beds

### 2 Baths

### 1,668SqFt

### Active

Step into luxury living in one of Reno’s most desirable 55+ communities. This like-new 2 bed, 2 bath Cambria Elite home features a rare 3-car garage and high-end finishes throughout. Designed for comfort and style, the open layout is perfect for everyday living and entertaining. Outside your door, literally, Regency offers unmatched amenities— pickleball to pools, clubs to community events—creates the perfect blend of activity, connection, and elevated lifestyle. ASSUMABLE VA LOAN! Call for details!

### 5 Beds

### 2 Baths

### 2,273SqFt

### Active

Centrally located and peacefully tucked at the end of a quiet cul-de-sac, this spacious 5-bedroom, 2.5-bath home sits on a generous 1/3-acre lot in the heart of Reno. Shaded by mature sycamore trees, the property offers central heating and A/C, a cozy fireplace with insert, updated kitchen cabinets and countertops, and solar panels supporting the water heater.

### 2 Beds

### 2 Baths

### 1,032SqFt

### Active

Open floor plan with vaulted ceilings in the living room, open kitchen with breakfast bar, separate laundry room and 2 bedrooms at opposite ends of home. HOA includes a pool, recreation room, and basketball court. Gated community conveniently located near the freeway, shopping centers, and plenty of restaurants to choose from. Close to the University and downtown.

### Active

One-of-a-Kind Ranch Retreat in One of Reno’s Most Coveted Neighborhoods, Juniper Hills! Discover a rare opportunity to own a stunning, single-level ranch-style home nestled on just under 2 acres of serene privacy. Located in one of Reno’s most sought-after neighborhoods, with no HOA, this expansive property offers the perfect blend of refined indoor living and exceptional outdoor space.

\[OPEN HOUSE: Friday 5/2 from 3-5pm and Saturday 5/3 from 1-3pm\] Discover this highly sought-after 4-bedroom, 2-bathroom home nestled just off Wilbur May in Double Diamond of South Reno. Step inside to a welcoming formal dining room and sitting area, perfect for entertaining or relaxing. The heart of the home features an open-concept layout connecting the kitchen, breakfast nook, and living room complete with a fireplace.

### 4 Beds

### 4 Baths

### 6,733SqFt

### Active

Highly desired, and rarely obtainable residence on over 2 acres is now offered to market. The distinguished community of Arrowcreek is known for it's resort style living in this privately guard gated community. Adjacent to BLM land and securely within the additional private gates of "The Reserve", this grand residence lies at the peak of one of the most sought after locations in the community. Full continuous city, valley & mountain views are enjoyed from every room and view point of the property.

### 3 Beds

### 2 Baths

### 4,322SqFt

### Active

Welcome to this stunning 4,322 sq. ft. custom home nestled in the coveted Galena Forest Estates, offering the perfect balance of mountain tranquility and modern convenience. This beautifully designed 3 bedroom, 2.5 bathroom home features an open-concept layout, high ceilings, and expansive windows that fill the space with natural light and breathtaking forest views. The kitchen seamlessly flows into the spacious living and dining areas, creating an ideal setting for both entertaining and everyday living.

### 3 Beds

### 2 Baths

### 1,933SqFt

### Active

Location AND convenience! Minutes from shopping/downtown/open space/the river & more. Easy I80 access for a quick drive to Truckee & beyond. Single level home w/ new interior paint & updated kitchen. Open floorplan, office in addition to bedroom count, separation between primary & secondary bedrooms. Low maintenance landscape, updated hardscape, patio cover, mountain views, covered entry, extended driveway space. A perfect blend of indoor/outdoor living. Buyer to verify all. Agents see private remarks.

# Northwest Reno Homes for Sale: 4028 Royal Sage 89503 (Video)

October 10, 2012 | Category: [Featured Listings](https://www.reno-realty.com/category/featured-listings/) | Tags: [89503 homes for sale reno](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/89503-homes-for-sale-reno/), [89523 homes for sale reno](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/89523-homes-for-sale-reno/), [large back yard reno homes](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/large-back-yard-reno-homes/), [mcqueen high school zoned homes for sale](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/mcqueen-high-school-zoned-homes-for-sale/), [northwest reno homes for sale](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/northwest-reno-homes-for-sale/), [regular sale nw reno nv](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/regular-sale-nw-reno-nv/), [renown reno real estate](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/renown-reno-real-estate/)

For further information watch the video below and click on the picture link at the bottom of the page for listing information.

![](https://www.reno-realty.com/wp-content/uploads/2012/10/backyard1-1.jpg "northwest reno homes for sale")

Turn-key regular sale in Northwest Reno ready for a quick close.

No front neighbors make for a quiet street with mountain views. Entering the home you’ll notice the handscraped laminate flooring throughout. Upon entry you’ll find the relaxing family room separated for privacy. The great room flows to the kitchen with ample cupboard space along with breakfast nook and easy access to the backyard. Surround sound is built in for the master bedroom and areas.

The master bedroom on the backside of the house is a retreat from a long day and activities of the front living areas and includes surround s. A fully landscaped backyard is ready for entertaining, playing, winding down after a long day.

The approximate 940 sqft. deck with overhang offers mountain views above mature trees. Add in the dog run and horseshoe pit and your Saturdays will be well spent relaxing.

https://www.reno-realty.com/ a-ykRsIY8nU” height=”360″ width=”640″ frameborder=”0″>

Reno-Realty.com Privacy Policy

This privacy policy has been compiled to better serve those who are concerned with how their ‘Personally Identifiable Information’ (PII) is being used online. PII, as described in US privacy law and information security, is information that can be used on its own or with other information to identify, contact, or locate a single person, or to identify an individual in context. Please read our privacy policy carefully to get a clear understanding of how we collect, use, protect or otherwise handle your Personally Identifiable Information in accordance with our website.

**What personal information do we collect from the people that visit our blog, website or app?**

When ordering, or registering, or requesting more information on our site, as appropriate, you may be asked to enter your name, email address, mailing address, phone number or other details to help you with your experience.

**When do we collect information?**

We collect information from you when you register on our site, subscribe to a newsletter, respond to a survey, fill out a form or enter information on our site.

**How do we use your information?**

We may use the information we collect from you when you register, make a purchase, sign up for our newsletter, respond to a survey or marketing communication, surf the website, or use certain other site features in the following ways:

**•** To administer a contest, promotion, survey or other site feature.  
**•** To ask for ratings and reviews of services or products  
**•** For Reno Realty marketing purposes.  
**•** To follow up with you after correspondence (live chat, email or phone inquiries)

**How do we protect your information?**

We do not use vulnerability scanning and/or scanning to PCI standards.  
We only provide articles and information. We never ask for credit card numbers directly.  
We use regular Malware Scanning.

**Do we use ‘cookies’?**

We do use cookies for tracking and marketing purposes.

-   Our third party vendors, including (but not limited to) Google, use cookies to serve ads based on your prior visits to our website or other websites.
-   Google’s and our other third party vendors use of advertising cookies enables them and their partners to serve ads to you based on your visit to our sites and/or other sites on the Internet.
-   Users may opt out of personalized advertising with Google by visiting [Ads Settings](https://www.google.com/settings/ads). Other third-party vendor’s use of cookies for personalized advertising can be opted out by visiting [www.aboutads.info](http://www.aboutads.info/choices/).

You can choose to have your computer warn you each time a cookie is being sent, or you can choose to turn off all cookies. You do this through your browser settings. Since each browser is a little different, look at your browser’s Help Menu to learn the correct way to modify your cookies.

If you turn cookies off, Some of the features that make your site experience more efficient may not function properly. Disabling cookies may make your site experience less efficient and our website may not function properly.

**Third-party disclosure**

We do not sell, trade, or otherwise transfer to outside parties your Personally Identifiable Information outside of Reno Realty.

**Third-party links**

We do include or offer third-party products or services on our website.

**We have implemented the following:**

We, along with third-party vendors such as Google use first-party cookies (such as the Google Analytics cookies) and third-party cookies (such as the DoubleClick cookie) or other third-party identifiers together to compile data regarding user interactions with ad impressions and other ad service functions as they relate to our website.

**Opting out:**  
Users can set preferences for how Google advertises to you using the Google Ad Settings page. Alternatively, you can opt out by visiting the Network Advertising Initiative Opt Out page or by using the Google Analytics Opt Out Browser add on.

**California Online Privacy Protection Act**

CalOPPA is the first state law in the nation to require commercial websites and online services to post a privacy policy. The law’s reach stretches well beyond California to require any person or company in the United States (and conceivably the world) that operates websites collecting Personally Identifiable Information from California consumers to post a conspicuous privacy policy on its website stating exactly the information being collected and those individuals or companies with whom it is being shared. – See more at: [http://consumercal.org/california-online-privacy-p…](http://consumercal.org/california-online-privacy-protection-act-caloppa/#sthash.0FdRbT51.dpuf)

**According to CalOPPA, we agree to the following:**

Users can visit our site anonymously.  
Once this privacy policy is created, we will add a link to it on our home page or as a minimum, on the first significant page after entering our website.

Our Privacy Policy link includes the word ‘Privacy’ and can easily be found on the page specified above.

You will be notified of any Privacy Policy changes:  
**•** On our Privacy Policy Page

Can change your personal information:  
**•** By emailing us  
**•** By calling us

**How does our site handle Do Not Track signals?**

We honor Do Not Track signals and Do Not Track, plant cookies, or use advertising when a Do Not Track (DNT) browser mechanism is in place.

**Does our site allow third-party behavioral tracking?**

It’s also important to note that we do not allow third-party behavioral tracking, but may use behavioral tracking to better understand our customer experience.

**COPPA (Children Online Privacy Protection Act)**

When it comes to the collection of personal information from children under the age of 13 years old, the Children’s Online Privacy Protection Act (COPPA) puts parents in control. The Federal Trade Commission, United States’ consumer protection agency, enforces the COPPA Rule, which spells out what operators of websites and online services must do to protect children’s privacy and safety online.

We do not specifically market to children under the age of 13 years old.

**Fair Information Practices**

The Fair Information Practices Principles form the backbone of privacy law in the United States and the concepts they include have played a significant role in the development of data protection laws around the globe. Understanding the Fair Information Practice Principles and how they should be implemented is critical to comply with the various privacy laws that protect personal information.

**In order to be in line with Fair Information Practices we will take the following responsive action, should a data breach occur:**  
We will notify you via email  
**•** Within 7 business days

We also agree to the Individual Redress Principle which requires that individuals have the right to legally pursue enforceable rights against data collectors and processors who fail to adhere to the law. This principle requires not only that individuals have enforceable rights against data users, but also that individuals have recourse to courts or government agencies to investigate and/or prosecute non-compliance by data processors.

**CAN SPAM Act**

The CAN-SPAM Act is a law that sets the rules for commercial email, establishes requirements for commercial messages, gives recipients the right to have emails stopped from being sent to them, and spells out tough penalties for violations.

**We collect your email address in order to:**  
**•** Send information, respond to inquiries, and/or other requests or questions  
**•** Market to our mailing list or continue to send emails to our clients after the original transaction has occurred.

**To be in accordance with CANSPAM, we agree to the following:**  
**•** Not use false or misleading subjects or email addresses.  
**•** Identify the message as an advertisement in some reasonable way.  
**•** Include the physical address of our business or site headquarters.  
**•** Monitor third-party email marketing services for compliance, if one is used.  
**•** Honor opt-out/unsubscribe requests quickly.  
**•** Allow users to unsubscribe by using the link at the bottom of each email.  
**  
If at any time you would like to unsubscribe from receiving future emails, you can email us at info@reno-realty.com OR:**  
**•** Follow the instructions at the bottom of each email and we will promptly remove you from **ALL** correspondence.

**Contacting Us**

If there are any questions regarding this privacy policy, you may contact us using the information below.

# Bronze Service Package

March 13, 2017

[Meet the Property Manager](https://www.reno-realty.com/our-agents/kristopher-kent/)

[Get a Free Rental Analysis](https://www.reno-realty.com/free-rental-analysis/)

[Management Packages](https://www.reno-realty.com/rentals/reno-property-management/)

  

Bronze Package

**This is our barebones package for the do-it-yourself Enthusiast**  
Base Fee: 5% of the monthly rental rate  
_**Services Included:**_

-   Monthly Rent Collection
-   24 Hour Facilitation of Maintenance & Repairs 1
-   ACH Direct Deposit of Rental Income
-   24/7 Access Internet Landlord Portal
-   Simplified Year End Accounting Statements
-   Portal Communication 2

_**Optional Fee for Service Items:**_

-   Tenant Placement: $495.00 3
-   Lease Renewal: $249.95 3
-   Drive-by Inspection: $49.95 4
-   Interior Inspection: $89.95 5
-   Eviction Processing: Initial Paperwork: $249.95 (no court); $495.00 (court appearance);  Lockout $149.95;  Personal Property Eviction: $149.95 6
-   All other services and requests are considered extraordinary and will be billed at an hourly rate of $95.00.

_**Requirements:**_

-   Must deposit and maintain a Repair/Expense Reserve of $1,000.00.
-   Minimum 2 Year Service Contract 7
-   Property must be “Rent Ready” 8

  

[Get Started](https://www.reno-realty.com/property-management-request/)

  

##### 1 _Maintenance & Repairs are billed separately in addition to service fee._

##### 2 _Unlimited portal communication, all other types of communication will be billed at the extraordinary hourly rate._

##### 3 _This is a fee for services product (additional cost), valid for 6 months or until a tenant is placed, whichever occurs first. Fees must be paid in advance, services included are Advertising on MLS, Zillow, 1 Craigslist Posting, Hot Pads and Trulia included for up to 6 months, screening of up to 5 groups of tenants. If the property is not rented within 6 months, you may elect to renew for $249.95 for an additional 6 month period. Showings will be self-showing, through a Rently lockbox._

##### 4  _This is a fee for services product (additional cost), which includes a drive by of the property, photos of the front, 2 sides and the address and a brief report of what was observed during the inspection. This information is placed in a pdf report and delivered via the communication portal._

##### 5 _This is a fee for services product (additional cost), Interior inspection includes a walk through of all bedrooms and bathrooms, garage, front and back yards and photos which are placed in a brief report of what was observed during the inspection._  _This information is placed in a pdf report and delivered via the communication portal._ 

##### 6 _Evictions require additional fees for process servers marked up by 25%, Owner may, at their own discretion, chose to seek legal counsel for additional fees._ 

##### 7 _Early termination fees apply. A $99 processing fee plus one half of the total amount remaining within the two year period of the gross monthly rents. i.e. cancellation at year 1 on a rental that rents at $1,000 monthly would be charged a $99 processing fee plus one half of the remaining months of the contract, $300.00 for a total termination fee of $400.00._

##### 8 _“Rent Ready” is defined as a property that is ready for a tenant to occupy (or is already occupied by a tenant) that is not missing such things as window coverings, appliances, smoke alarms, CO Detectors, in need of repairs, in need of cleaning, etc. In the event that a property is not “Rent Ready” the owner will be billed at the extraordinary rate of $95 per hour to bring the property to a “rent ready” status in order to start the Bronze package._

May 25, 2017

[Management Packages](https://www.reno-realty.com/rentals/reno-property-management/)

  

Gold Package

**This is our _MOST POPULAR_ package for the Seasoned Investor**  
Base Fee: 10% of the monthly rental rate  
_**Services Included:**_

-   1% Discount on Property Sales.
-   Quarterly Broker Price Opinion of Value Report
-   Quarterly Drive-by Inspections
-   Tenant Placement
-   Placement Advertising
-   Monthly Rent Collection
-   24 Hour Facilitation of Maintenance & Repairs 1
-   ACH Direct Deposit of Rental Income
-   24/7 Access Internet Landlord Portal
-   Simplified Year End Accounting Statements
-   Portal Communication 2

_**Optional Fee for Service Items:**_

-   Tenant Placement: $495.00 3
-   Lease Renewal: $249.95 3
-   Drive-by Inspection: $49.95 4
-   Interior Inspection: $89.95 5
-   All other services and requests are considered extraordinary and will be billed at an hourly rate of $95.00.

_**Requirements:**_

-   Must deposit and maintain a Repair/Expense Reserve of $500.00.
-   Minimum 1 Year Service Contract 5
-   Property must be “Rent Ready” 6

[Get Started](https://www.reno-realty.com/property-management-request/)

  

##### 1 _Maintenance & Repairs are billed separately in addition to service fee._

##### 2 _Unlimited portal communication, all other types of communication will be billed at the extraordinary hourly rate._

##### 3 _This is a fee for services product (additional cost), valid for 6 months or until a tenant is placed, whichever occurs first. Fees must be paid in advance, services included are Advertising on MLS, Zillow, 1 Craigslist Posting, Hot Pads and Trulia included for up to 6 months, screening of up to 5 groups of tenants. If the property is not rented within 6 months, you may elect to renew for $249.95 for an additional 6 month period._

##### 4  _This is a fee for services product (additional cost), which includes a drive by of the property, photos of the front, 2 sides and the address and a brief report of what was observed during the inspection. This information is placed in a pdf report and delivered via the communication portal._

##### 5 _This is a fee for services product (additional cost), Interior inspection includes a walk through of all bedrooms and bathrooms, garage, front and back yards and photos which are placed in a brief report of what was observed during the inspection._  _This information is placed in a pdf report and delivered via the communication portal._ 

##### 6 _“Rent Ready” is defined as a property that is ready for a tenant to occupy (or is already occupied by a tenant) that is not missing such things as window coverings, appliances, smoke alarms, CO Detectors, in need of repairs, in need of cleaning, etc. In the event that a property is not “Rent Ready” the owner will be billed at the extraordinary rate of $95 per hour to bring the property to a “rent ready” status in order to start the Bronze package._

10% of Gross Monthly Rents (1-year minimum contract)  
Repair/Expense Reserve: $500.00

Services Included:

Tenant Placement\*  
Monthly Rent Collection  
24 Hour Facilitation of Maintenance & Repairs\*  
Online Landlord Portal & Tenant Portal  
Simplified Year End Accounting Statement  
Portal Communication\*\*  
ACH Direct Deposit of Rental Income  
Lease Renewals  
Move-In/Move-Out

Optional Fee for Service Items:  
Eviction Processing: $249.95 (no court)\*\*\*\*\* $495.00 (court appearance)\*\*\*\*\* Lockout $149.95\*\*\*\*\*\* Personal Property Eviction: $149.95  
Additional Property Inspection Reports: $95 Per Inspection\*\*\*\*  
Extraordinary services provided are billed at an hourly rate of $95.00.

\*Maintenance & Repairs are billed separately in addition to service fee.  
\*\*Calls are billed at $19.95 per call plus $9.95 per each additional 15 minute period (rounded up), text messages are billed at $1.99 per each inbound and outbound message. Emails are billed at $19.95 per email plus $9.95 per each additional 15 minute period (rounded up) for reading and responding to email communication.  
\*\*\*Must be paid in advance. Advertising on MLS, Zillow, 1 Craigslist Posting, Hot Pads and Trulia included for 6 months, if at the end of the 6 month term the property is not rented, you may elect to renew for $249.95 for an additional 6 months.  
\*\*\*\*Paid in advance, report is a one page standard form regarding the condition of the property during tenancy.  
\*\*\*\*\*Fees are in addition to court fees, service of process fees.  
\*\*\*\*\*\*Fees for locksmith, process server are in addition to this fee.

3230 Hackamore Way is a gorgeous, full stucco, semi-custom built home you will not want to miss out on. It is conveniently located in a beautiful area in Winnemucca and is loaded with amenities. Its meticulously designed interior seamlessly blends sophistication with warmth and elegance with functionality. The home has a "great room" design with an open floor plan yet tastefully features a formal sitting area/living room just off of the entry. Attractively designed, the kitchen features rich, handsome

# Why Reno Realty

January 8, 2015

Clients matter to Us! Why [Reno Realty](https://www.reno-realty.com)? We treat you with respect, listen to what you need and want and deliver results. Our brokerage provides a culture of respect, integrity and synergy, our Realtors work together to make sure you get what you need, whether it is getting that home sold or bought, we represent buyers and sellers aggressively and to the point, real estate reinvigorated.

# 2114 Fort Bridger Road Fernley, NV 89408

May 21, 2013 | Category: [Featured Listings](https://www.reno-realty.com/category/featured-listings/) | Tags: [fernley nv](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/fernley-nv/), [fernley nv homes for sale](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/fernley-nv-homes-for-sale/), [fernley nv mls listings](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/fernley-nv-mls-listings/), [fernley real estate for sale](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/fernley-real-estate-for-sale/), [fernley seller listing agent broker](https://www.reno-realty.com/tag/fernley-seller-listing-agent-broker/)

Just listed! Regular sale in Fernley, NV.

Built in 2004 with newer laminate flooring installed. RV access to the side and backyard includes a large storage shed. Additional concrete slabs in driveway for more room.

Both front and back yard are limited maintenance for ease of living.

The kitchen flows to the living room which boasts a ceiling fan and gas fireplace for comfort. The master has an additional walk out to the backyard and the two additional bedrooms are hidden away from the living room for those quiet times.

For more information contact Broker Kristopher Kent at (775) 870-4280 and Kris@Reno-Realty.com and watch the video below.
